Load JSON data\n    with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    print(f\"Loaded JSON data: {data}\")\n\n    # Extract HWPX to temp directory\n    temp_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_replaced'\n    if os.path.exists(temp_dir):\n        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)\n    os.makedirs(temp_dir)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, 'r') as zin:\n        zin.extractall(temp_dir)\n\n    # Define namespaces (found in the XML)\n    namespaces = {\n        'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\n    }\n\n    # Register namespaces to preserve them during write\n    for prefix, uri in namespaces.items():\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n    # Also register other namespaces that might be in the files\n    ET.register_namespace('opf', 'http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf')\n\n    # Track which files were modified\n    modified_files = []\n    replacements_made = []\n\n    # Walk through all files in the extracted directory\n    for root_dir, dirs, files in os.walk(temp_dir):\n        for fname in files:\n            fpath = os.path.join(root_dir, fname)\n\n            # Only process XML files\n            if not fname.endswith('.xml'):\n                continue\n\n            with open(fpath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                content = f.read()\n\n            # Check if file contains any placeholders\n            if '{{' not in content:\n                continue\n\n            print(f\"\\nProcessing file: {fname}\")\n            print(f\"Content before:\\n{content[:500]}...\")\n\n            # Parse the XML\n            tree = ET.parse(fpath)\n            xml_root = tree.getroot()\n\n            # Find all paragraphs\n            # Using the namespace-aware findall\n            ns = {'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'}\n\n            # Find all hp:p elements (paragraphs)\n            paragraphs = xml_root.findall('.//hp:p', ns)\n            print(f\"Found {len(paragraphs)} paragraphs\")\n\n            file_modified = False\n\n            for para in paragraphs:\n                # Find all text runs (hp:run/hp:t) within this paragraph\n                text_elements = []\n                # Get all hp:run elements, then their hp:t children\n                for run in para.findall('.//hp:run', ns):\n                    for t in run.findall('hp:t', ns):\n                        text_elements.append(t)\n\n                if not text_elements:\n                    continue\n\n                #
